### 197 subject events

- **1915.07** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  摔伤之后两天，房东女人在门厅拦住Katharina Bauer，楼里的Josef Bauer正提着菜篮站在门边。房东女人说楼梯间不许孩子嬉闹，铁扶手磕坏要赔。Katharina Bauer把裹着布条的Klara往身后一拉，回了句“灯都没有，谁看得见台阶”。房东女人把话记在房租本边上，说下回修灯的钱从房租里扣。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.07; flag +stairs_accident_noted_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Klara拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Mitzi Stein弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Klara接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1918.08** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环滚进沟沿，Klara扑过去抓，膝盖先着了地。Mitzi Lechner把铁环拎起来，在手里转了一圈，说不能白捡。Klara摸出一颗石弹子递过去，Mitzi Lechner这才松手。回家路上Klara一瘸一拐，裤膝上沾着石屑和血，经过Mitzi Lechner家门口时特意绕到街对面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Klara的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Klara在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

  Klara请楼下的Emil Winter白天来照看Katharina Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Emil Winter上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Klara下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Katharina Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1938.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Alois Lechner对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.07999999999999996; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”; flag +faction_grew_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004515_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005565_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Elisabeth Moser 3在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Klara排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-divorce/partner-moves-to-shared-housing` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation, household
  Katharina Kral 3把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Klara留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Klara在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.020000000000000018; role_

- **1938.01** `r15-wish.power-intrigue::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/outmaneuvered` tags: politics, power, wish, intrigue, office, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Kral 2在会议上先一步拿到了多数。Klara的提案被改成一段附注，连名字都没有留下。散会后，Klara在走廊里站了一会儿，然后去下一间办公室。
  _effects: reputation -0.050000000000000044; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:141_

  Klara把那段关系继续藏在日常的缝隙里。一次擦肩、一张没有署名的纸条、借来的半个钟头，都足够撑过这个月；回家前Klara在街角站了一会儿，把围巾重新系好。
  _effects: state_parameter forbidden-attachment.exposure 0 -> 0; state_parameter forbidden-attachment.reciprocity 53 -> 56; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.01** `r13-edu.sell-books::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cheap-sale-for-coal` tags: education, books, sale, cash, study, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara急等钱用，Anton Hofer只肯给两克朗，说这些书明年就改版。Klara咬着牙卖了，回家路上用那点钱买了面粉和煤，剩下的塞进母亲的围裙口袋。那天晚上家里生了火，Klara却觉得比平时冷。母亲把围裙口袋缝了两针，没问钱是哪来的。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉的课本); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  Klara把地窖里的猎枪用油布包好，埋进煤堆最底下。Luise Hofer来检查时，用刺刀在煤堆上捅了两下，Klara站在旁边，手里端着一壶给士兵的热水。刺刀拔出来时，刀尖上只有黑灰。
  _effects: health -0.019999999999999997 (门厅里的异国军靴); condition; flag +hid_weapon_from_occupation; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; knowledge “the officer probes the coal heap but not the cellar floor”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

  _effects: organization_funds; reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; flag +bribed_auditor; knowledge “审计报告里的“重复报销”被改成了“记账口径不一”，实习证明由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  铅笔头短得捏不住，Klara把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1940.07** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara和Josef Berg谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Klara家，钥匙只在白天给。Josef Berg说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Rosa Lechner听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Klara把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73_

- **1950.08** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.dispute-rate` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Berta Winter用拇指甲刮着一件领子的边线，说这儿跳了针。Klara把煤油灯往前推了推，让他凑近看，说线是细了点，活没少做。Elisabeth Hofer倚在门框上没走，屋里一时只有尺子碰桌面的响。
  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000671:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.08** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara上楼敲了Elisabeth Berg的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Elisabeth Berg把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Klara能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1951.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-keys` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  移交清单少了一把储藏室的钥匙。Klara没有提，Helene Bauer也没有问；三个月后，清洁工在储藏室发现两箱以Klara名义订阅的行业期刊，收件地址仍是这间办公室。
  _effects: position_end 委员会主席 @org-sdap; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001147:child; flag +kept_office_keys; knowledge “储藏室有两箱以我的名义订阅的期刊，收件地址仍是这间办公室。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Ferdinand Hofer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Klara把床单对折，腾出一半，Ferdinand Hofer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1951.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把Grete Kral和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Klara把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1951.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Klara当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000912:child_

- **1952.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/mistaken-identity` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, postwar
  一个代表团走错了房间，把Klara当成新来的部长，围着说了十分钟的请求。Klara没有纠正。等人走后，Klara坐在那张不属于自己的椅子上，听见走廊里有人在找真正的部长。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; reputation 0.025000000000000022; state_add stage-king state:world-vienna-1912:292; flag +mistaken_for_official; flag +stage_king; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。

- **1952.03** `r3-mig.political-exile::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/met-compatriot-in-exile` tags: migration, exile, politics, safe_house, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil Lechner认出了Klara，在楼梯间低声问家里怎么样。Klara说不知道，出来以后没有收到信。Emil Lechner把半包烟塞过来，说“别在餐厅吃饭，那里有人记名字”。Klara把烟收下，没有告诉他自己的真名。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +met_compatriot_in_exile; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002121:child; knowledge “the hotel restaurant is watched”_

- **1952.04** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.labour-exchange/exchange-trial-work` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, labour_exchange, bureaucracy
  介绍所里那个Katharina Berg 3说木牌上的地址多半过时了，不如自己去问。Klara走了四十分钟到洗衣房，院子里晾着床单。老板娘让她先熨一件试试，看完说留下吧，明天还这个时辰来。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154_

- **1952.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/turn-away-with-fare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara直说家里腾不出地方，从抽屉里摸出半克朗车费和一块黑面包，塞给Anna Lechner。Anna Lechner的藤箱提手已经磨毛，在门槛上磕了一下。Klara关上门，听见脚步声沿楼梯往下，看门人在楼下问了一句，Anna Lechner答得含含糊糊；厨房里少了一块面包，也少了一个本该住下的人。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (给表亲车费); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001504:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-favor-was-already-owed` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Otto Winter 2早在Klara上任前就挂在一个“临时调查员”的名下，工资由上一任签字。Klara不能新设岗位，只能补一份追认函；函上写明“工作内容另行通知”，而通知一直没有发出。
  _effects: flag +retroactive_sinecure; knowledge “临时调查员的岗位是上一任留下的，工作内容从未书面通知。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: reputation 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011827_

  姐姐家在多瑙河对岸，院子里晾着床单。孩子在那里住了一个月，回来时脸圆了些，手里攥着一块河边的白石子。可他站在门口不肯叫Klara，姐姐蹲下来哄，Klara把行李提进屋，听见身后孩子叫了一声姨。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1953.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  护士把冰袋按在Klara额上，水顺着鬓角流进衣领。Otto Winter 2坐在床脚，数着窗外渐渐亮起的铺面门板，说回去给炉子添块煤。隔壁床的老头整夜在喊一个女人的名字。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧)_

- **1953.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖（其中0.03克朗记入债务）)_

- **1953.09** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  椅子叠到一半，Josef Lechner压低声音说今晚有三个人没投票就走了。Klara把最后一块瓜子壳扫进簸箕，说记住那三张脸，比上台多说一句有用。灯熄时大厅只剩木头发出的轻响。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  第一次会议只来了三个人，其中一个是房东，来问房租。Klara把章程念完，没有人反对，也没有人加入。办公室的钥匙还在，但那间屋子比街上更安静。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  办事处后门的Ferdinand 2把一份旧名册翻到空页，让Klara在“会长”后面签下名字。三天后，Klara真的收到一枚铜钥匙和一张聘书：夜校同学会会长，职责是保管储物间。同学会只有三个成员，储物间的锁已经坏了。Ferdinand 2没有再出现。

  _effects: state_parameter stage-sovereign.publicBelief 20 -> 12; state_parameter stage-sovereign.scandal 0 -> 6_

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1955.07** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); flag +watch_pawned_

- **1955.12** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-seal-was-used` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Gustav Hofer用Klara留在抽屉里的印章签了一份采购单，把库房里最后一批煤卖给了城外的砖厂。Klara发现时，账已经做平，签字栏里是Klara自己的名字。Gustav Hofer说只是替Klara省了麻烦，还把那支用旧的印泥放回了原处。
  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱); reputation -0.09999999999999998; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand_

  _effects: organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:344; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1957.01** `kernel-family.inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/inheritance.older-will` tags: task, task:family, family, inheritance, siblings, estate, law, vienna
  Helene Bauer从行李里掏出另一张更早的遗嘱，说工具和存款都该归长子。Ferdinand 2把两张纸并排放好，说私人纠纷要去法院；诉讼费三十克朗，Klara拿不出。遗产在办事处冻结了半年，Klara靠替人浆洗撑过冬天。

  补考那天，Mitzi在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Franz Lechner跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Mitzi去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner; education apprenticeship_

  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance)_

  _effects: flag +returned_to_school; education secondary_

  Mitzi只把Rosa送到巷口的煤油灯下就折回去。第二天在水泵边，Rosa学Mitzi走路的样子，几个人跟着笑，Mitzi拎起水桶没有接话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  _effects: cash -0.1 (只交了一个月会费); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: cash 8 (第一个月工资全部交给家里); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62_

  _effects: reputation -0.04; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1929.11** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition

  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1930.01** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family

  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

  问到后来，Alois Lechner提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Katharina Berg把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Katharina Berg说不是Mitzi的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Mitzi心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  _effects: cash -4 (当场付下定金和钥匙钱); residence_

  衙门走廊的地砖是黑白相间的，Mitzi站着站着就把重心从一只脚换到另一只脚。窗口里的办事员姓什么没人知道，只叫“三号窗”。表递进去时，里面说“等复核”，声音很平。
  _effects: health -0.03 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; skill_

  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.019999999999999962_

  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; unemployment (left the Vienna factory); cash -8 (train ticket and lodgings（其中7克朗记入债务）)_

- **1934.01** `p10-migration-tasks.find-lodging/lodging-no-guarantor` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, lodging, arrival
  看门人看了他的身份证，说三处房都要担保人签字，他一个也拿不出。房东把地址纸条还给他，他只好拖着箱子去车站候车室的长椅上过夜，半夜被巡夜人叫醒查了两次身份证。第二天他再去门房，看门人只摇头。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1934.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.wire-fix` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Mitzi把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1934.07** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/call-doctor` tags: health, family, home
  夜里Josef Lechner哭醒，Mitzi独自用湿布敷额，熬到街上有人扫雪才去请医生。医生进门时靴底带着雪泥，听完胸口说无大碍；那三克朗花得心疼，却换回一条稳下来的呼吸。
  _effects: health 0.1 (医生诊治后退烧); cash -3 (支付医生诊费和药粉（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1935.01** `p10-edu-work-tasks.seek-apprenticeship/p10-edu-work-tasks.seek-apprenticeship.test-piece` tags: adulthood, education, work, task, night_school, apprenticeship, career, craft
  作坊里的学徒都在看。他把第三个榫头装上去，木槌敲了两下才到位。Emil Moser 2说带一个来，明天。他把废料收进工具袋，出了门才发现袖子沾满木屑。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1935.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Mitzi在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中12克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1935.08** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg生下一个男婴，取名Otto Bauer。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Emil把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Mitzi躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:114_

- **1935.09** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Mitzi对Katharina Kral 2说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Katharina Kral 2把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Mitzi的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Mitzi把Otto Bauer的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Otto Bauer今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Mitzi听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 28; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Katharina Berg帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Mitzi点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:004855; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1936.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Emil Moser 2排在前面，回头替Mitzi看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Mitzi用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1936.06** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Mitzi点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Elisabeth Bauer量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Mitzi在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg生下一个女婴，取名Luise Bauer。接生的人收六克朗，Emil在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Mitzi起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:132_

- **1937.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.talk-tram` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Mitzi把报纸推到一边，和Grete Moser聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Mitzi起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (两杯咖啡的价钱); mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Luise Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Mitzi把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005730_

- **1937.04** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/give-place-to-mother` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Gustav Berg怀里的孩子脸烧得通红，Mitzi往后退了半步，把用粉笔写的号码塞进Gustav Berg手里。药房伙计收了六十赫勒，给一瓶褐色药水；回家路上Mitzi一阵阵发冷。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (街角药房买退烧药（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1937.08** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Mitzi在圣文生会门口登记，领到一张面包券和一小袋土豆。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1937.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi看Emil Kral盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Emil Kral愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Mitzi门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Mitzi打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; food_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Luise Kral 2在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Mitzi排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1938.01** `p10-edu-work-tasks.apply-scholarship/p10-edu-work-tasks.apply-scholarship.rejected` tags: adulthood, education, work, task, night_school, apprenticeship, career, scholarship
  官员翻完工钱单，说家庭收入证明由房东签字不足为凭，还要户籍局盖章。申请被退回，理由写在纸条上：收入材料不充分。他当天傍晚去问夜校，簿记班的学费低得多，也收工龄够的人。他把那张退回的纸条留作书签。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; knowledge “基金会的收入证明必须由户籍局盖章，不接受房东签字的收据”; flag +night_school_applicant_

- **1938.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Emil说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Emil陪着Mitzi去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1938.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Mitzi攥紧扶手。Gustav Berg站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中3克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: cash -4 (被征用的粮食折价（其中4克朗记入债务）); flag +requisition_receipt; knowledge “征用收据只在下一批检查前有效。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  收容所门前排的队比平时多了一倍，Maria Kral把两张床并成一张，让两个女人各睡一头。Mitzi半夜醒来，发现旁边的人正把毯子往自己这边拉；两人谁也没有说话，最后各盖一半。
  _effects: cash -1 (收容所门口的队伍（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +shared_a_shelter_bed; role; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; move place-wien_

- **1939.08** `r14-hist.denunciation-letter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/letter-mailed-before-curfew` tags: denunciation, repression, betrayal, neighbor, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Mitzi在厨房桌上写了两页信，说楼下的Marie Huber夜里有陌生人来，窗帘一直拉到天亮。信寄到警察局，Luise Kral 2在收文簿上盖了日期，转给街区负责人。三天后，Marie Huber被带走问话；Mitzi在楼梯间遇见Marie Huber的妹妹，说最近治安不好，晚上别开窗。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +wrote_denunciation_letter; knowledge “the denunciation letter was forwarded to the block warden”_

- **1939.08** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction

  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006332_

- **1939.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Franz Lechner从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person-franz-lechner_

- **1940.01** `r13-civ.quarantine::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.quarantine-crisis/crisis-lodger-fever` tags: health, quarantine, epidemic, neighbor, confinement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil半夜敲墙，说同屋的人烧得说胡话。Mitzi隔着板壁问要不要去叫医生，Emil说医生来了也只给退热粉，还要收出诊费。天亮时，楼梯间多了一股石灰水味，Luise Hofer把白线画到了隔壁门口。
  _effects: cash -2 (门框上的石灰线（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; flag +crisis_quarantine_house; knowledge “a second household on the stair was placed under quarantine”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1940.08** `r3-war.refugee-train::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/blocked-car-door` tags: war, refugee, train, station, migration, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa Lechner说军人优先，把Emil的箱子踢到一旁。Mitzi把箱子重新拎起来，挡在车门前，说“她男人在前线”。Emil Stein看了看Emil怀里的孩子，把哨子含在嘴里没吹；火车动了，Rosa Lechner在后面记下车厢号。
  _effects: flag +blocked_car_door_for_refugee_family; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the stationmaster recorded the carriage number”_

- **1940.08** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Mitzi端着一只旧锅去敲Emil的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Emil让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Mitzi连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.12** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Katharina Berg在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/enrolls` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi带着Otto Bauer去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Otto Bauer一直捏着那张课程表，Mitzi则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 36 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 44 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate); mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi被叫到学校，老师说Franz Bauer这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Mitzi没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  排练到一半，两个便衣推门进来，站在最后一排听。Emil没有停，只把一首哀歌换成进行曲。散场后，Emil让Mitzi把点名册带回家藏一夜，说Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens里最近多了几个不唱歌只记名字的人。
  _effects: flag +carried_choir_roll_home; role; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; organization_membership; knowledge “plainclothes men attend the choir rehearsals”_

- **1941.04** `fa17-studio.private-failure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-as-pulp` tags: art, writing, failure, poverty, privacy, craft, plagiarism, vienna
  Mitzi把没有写完的稿子卖给一家专门印消遣读物的小出版社，换来的钱刚好够付一个月房租。编辑把结尾改成了火车相撞，又在封面上印了一个陌生的笔名。Mitzi在报摊上看见那本册子时，先认出了自己写的一句雨，然后才认出那个名字不是自己的。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉残稿的稿费); state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:168; reputation 0.010000000000000002; knowledge “出版社把结尾改成了火车相撞，封面上的笔名不是我。”; flag +sold_under_pseudonym_

- **1941.06** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Mitzi按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform_

  Mitzi和Katharina Berg叫上楼下两家，一同去房东的事务所。Emil Stein隔着柜台听他们说完，翻出账簿，答应先补上楼梯间的窗闩，涨租从下下个月起算。四人出门时挤在台阶上，谁也没先说话，Katharina Berg把帽子往后一推，说总算没白跑。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  他把本子放在厂主桌上，Emil Stein伸手来翻，说字号不规整。声音传出门外，几个包装工停下手里的活。厂主最后把本子合上压在墨水瓶下，说夜班缺人。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000007; health -0.019999999999999997 (改上夜班，作息颠倒)_

- **1942.05** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/home-remedy` tags: health, family, home
  邻居过来看了一眼，教Mitzi用湿布敷额。Josef Lechner天亮时安静睡了，Mitzi却耽误半日活计；一克朗五十赫勒的工钱没了，床边的醋味却留了一整天。
  _effects: health 0.05 (家庭护理后烧略退); cash -1.5 (缺工半日被扣工钱（其中1.5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1942.05** `vienna-women-housework.care-old-parent/care.institution` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Mitzi跑了两趟区公所和医院，问清住院的费用，把家里存着的八克朗取出来又借了四克朗，才把Rosa Lechner送进病房。回来时屋子空了一半，她站在灶前，第一次不知道该先做什么。Katharina Berg说这样也好，至少夜里能睡整觉。
  _effects: cash -8 (支付母亲住院的押金与费用（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.019999999999999997 (夜间不再需要频繁起身，睡眠好转); mood -0.06000000000000005; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

  Mitzi夹着一叠印着候选人头像的传单，从巷口走到巷尾。有人在围裙上擦手，问候选人是不是也住这样的楼。Mitzi答不上来，只把传单往门缝里又塞了一张。天黑时，口袋里只剩三张。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +canvassed_election; role; skill_

- **1943.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Mitzi Lechner把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Mitzi Lechner在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1944.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-on-list` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Mitzi替Rosa Lechner把名字、出生年和最后住址写在纸上，递进窗口。Emil Stein翻了三本名册，手指停在一行被水洇开的字上，说这个人登记在临时收容所。Rosa Lechner听见时，手里的伞掉在地上；Mitzi替她把伞捡起来，才发现自己的手也在抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”_

- **1944.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Emil把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Mitzi说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Rosa Lechner在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Mitzi的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1944.04** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/hold-list-and-watch` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena把会费账本推回去，说外头那个组织答应给煤，只要名单。Mitzi把账本合上，用一块旧布包好塞进缝纫机底下，说先看看谁在往外递话。两人都没再提这事。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +kept_district_list_private_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

  Mitzi在厂门口签了名，第二天工头就把他调去最热的炉旁，说“既然有工夫搞组织”。Josef Moser说这是常事，让Mitzi先忍一个月。月底，Mitzi的加班被取消了。
  _effects: reputation -0.07999999999999999; flag +union_signature_blacklisted; knowledge “the foreman punishes union signatures with the worst furnace”_

- **1945.05** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi数出八赫勒交给Katharina Berg，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Katharina Berg说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; flag +funeral_society_member_

  Mitzi把行李捆在自行车后座上，趁夜往维也纳骑。路过村口时，Rosa Lechner站在石灰线后面看着，没有拦。Mitzi骑出两里路，才想起灶上还炖着一锅汤。
  _effects: flag +fled_typhus_epidemic; move place-wien_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Mitzi看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Mitzi等到夜里，从后院的柴门溜出去上工。车间里没有人问，机器照常转，只有换班的人往旁边让了半步。回家时白线还在，Mitzi跨过去，鞋底带回一层湿泥。
  _effects: cash 3 (门框上的石灰线); reputation -0.10000000000000003; flag +broke_quarantine; knowledge “the workshop does not ask why a quarantined worker returned”_

  Mitzi把十四克朗的整张票子压在药费单上，说这钱不用还，只要求母亲那间朝院子的屋子以后归自己照看，钥匙也归自己管。Anna Lechner的咖啡杯在桌上顿了一下，说照看可以，房契上的名字另说；药费单总算从糖罐底下抽了出来，兄妹之间却多了一层账。
  _effects: cash -14 (先垫付母亲全部药费（其中13克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001504:child; flag +claims_mother_room_

- **1946.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Mitzi摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Elisabeth Berg第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Mitzi只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

  Emil Stein说那封信毁了两个冬天：工作没了，孩子在学校被叫“叛徒的亲戚”。Mitzi想解释当时楼里有人被查夜，Emil Stein打断说“你至少可以敲门问一句”。Ferdinand 2把档案袋推到一边，说战后委员会只受理有签名的材料；Emil Stein拿起表，走了。

  Ferdinand 2把保险册摊开，在Mitzi的名字后面贴上一枚印花，又用黑笔填了工号和车间。Mitzi问生病了能领多少，Ferdinand 2说前六周按半薪，之后要医生证明。册子被收进铁柜，钥匙挂在Ferdinand 2腰上。
  _effects: cash -2 (疾病保险的印花); flag +enrolled_in_sickness_insurance; role; knowledge “sickness benefit pays half wages for the first six weeks”_

- **1947.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Katharina Berg借给Mitzi一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Mitzi睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1947.07** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi和Elisabeth Bauer一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Mitzi去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Mitzi先垫了药钱，说以后再说。

- **1949.03** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1949.06** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Rosa Lechner放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Berta Bauer在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Mitzi，Rosa Lechner把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003532:child; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; food_

- **1949.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Mitzi的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1949.07** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  Gustav Weiss说人死了会在天上重逢，Mitzi说没有证据，两个人从厨房吵到楼梯口。吵到最后，Gustav Weiss承认自己也不知道，只是愿意这么想；Mitzi靠在栏杆上笑了一下，说“那你刚才还说得那么肯定”。

- **1951.12** `statistical_death/pneumonia` tags: death
  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang发起高烧，咳嗽越来越深。邻居帮忙去请医生，医生到时只留下药粉和一张账单；几天后呼吸变得很浅。Katharina Berg在场。
  _effects: death (肺炎)_

- **1914.01** `statistical_infection`

- **1914.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1914.04** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  木马在Rosa手里还没捂热，就转到了弟妹怀里。Rosa从纸包底下抽出那本图画书，封面卷了角，边上有人用铅笔写过字。Alois Lechner看看木马，又看看书，把报纸叠好收进抽屉。那晚Rosa把书压在枕头下，借着窗缝的光翻了三页。
  _effects: relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.039999999999999994; flag +likes_reading_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Rosa的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Rosa在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1921.02** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  Rosa想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Alois Lechner听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Rosa试着站起来，被Alois Lechner按回台阶上。
  _effects: health -0.04 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Grete Moser家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Rosa递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Grete Moser端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Rosa把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1922.08** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  土豆从围裙里滚出来，Rosa蹲下去追，手心一松，三枚十克鲁泽的铜板叮叮当当掉在石板路上。两枚捡回来了，最后一枚滚进排水格栅，在铁条下转了个圈。Rosa趴下去够，指甲刮在铁条上。回家Alois Lechner数完钱，脸色沉下来，Rosa站在桌边不敢动。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.06_

  队伍拐弯时，Josef Moser朝Rosa招手。Rosa挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Rosa把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; skill_

  Franz Lechner走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Rosa把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Rosa坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-franz-lechner; skill; mood -0.05; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

  Rosa把煤油灯往桌子中间挪，让Otto Lechner看清算术本上的格子。孩子把七加八数了三遍，Rosa没有直接说答案，只把豆子一颗颗排在桌上。最后一页写完时，灯芯已经烧黑，Rosa才发现自己的作业还空着半页。

  你把已经掏出来的赫勒又放回口袋，说不赊了。老板哼了一声，把账本往柜台上一合。你拎着空筐跟邻居往火车站方向走，在货运站边的煤渣堆里捡了半袋碎煤和焦炭。手指冻得发僵，指甲缝里全是黑灰。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

  _effects: cash 8 (信里只写了一个数目); flag +borrowed_from_sibling; role; relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; knowledge “the sibling lent money without telling his wife”_

  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Grete Kral 2用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Rosa才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; flag +parish_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43_

  Josef Berg把搪瓷缸伸过来，说已经两天没找到活。Rosa把勺子沉到底，捞了稠的给他，后面立刻有人喊不公平。Elisabeth Berg把锅盖一扣，说再吵就都别吃。队伍安静下来，只剩勺子碰缸的声音。
  _effects: reputation 0.07999999999999999; flag +soup_kitchen_crisis_shift; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

  面粉最后在Anton Winter家的灶台下找到。Anton Winter说是孩子拖进去的，Rosa没有拆穿，只把口袋重新扎好。那天晚上，两家还是分吃了同一锅面糊，但Rosa记住了Anton Winter端碗时避开的目光。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +unforgotten_theft; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Grete Kral 2在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Grete Kral 2敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1934.07** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/call-doctor` tags: health, family, home

  _effects: health 0.1 (医生诊治后退烧); cash -3 (支付医生诊费和药粉（其中3克朗记入债务）); mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Rosa手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

  _effects: flag +taught_parent_to_write_name; role; skill; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; skill_

  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.04999999999999999; flag +accepted_hours_reform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金（其中9克朗记入债务）); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

  有人在门口喊，说汤是给本区人喝的，不能便宜了外来的。Rosa看了看锅里剩的底子，又看了看队伍里那些没票的人。Mitzi让Rosa把门关一半，说先紧着孩子和老人。门板合上时，外面有人用德语和另一种话一起骂。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg_

- **1913.07** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Katharina Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。

- **1915.12** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/landlord-complaint` tags: childhood, family, home, street

  “你看看这角。”Katharina Bauer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Emil伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Katharina Bauer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Emil手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Emil一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Maria Kral把Emil拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Emil把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Emil蹲下把两包并排，按Maria Kral说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1920.12** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Rosa把皇帝像在Emil眼前晃了晃。Emil数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Rosa已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Emil把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Grete Stein在绳子那头喊。Emil踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Grete Stein数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Emil把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Emil的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Emil在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Helene Bauer一挣，Emil只能把Helene Bauer抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Emil背着Helene Bauer在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Helene Bauer擦鼻涕。等Helene Bauer睡沉，Emil的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001147:child_

- **1923.08** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Emil每天放学都在柜台前站一会儿，看玻璃板底下压着的烟画，顺手把歪了的报纸摞齐。Lena擦完柜台，从抽屉最里面摸出一张揉皱的风景卡，边角还沾着烟丝，塞过来时说了句“别挡着门”。Emil把卡夹进课本，第二天带了一张重复的来给Lena看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1923.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Katharina Stein从讲台抽屉里拿出一本没有封面的书，书脊用线重新缝过。Emil答应只在课间看。第三天放学下起雨，书角在书包里洇湿。Emil回家用熨斗隔着旧布压了半晚，还书时把淋湿的那页也指给Katharina Stein看。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1924.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Emil把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Katharina Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Emil都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.04_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Ferdinand Winter 2把Emil叫到巷口，问Elisabeth Bauer叫什么、住在哪间屋。Emil答到一半就停住了。Ferdinand Winter 2说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Emil回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Elisabeth Bauer正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Emil。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

  _effects: mood -0.08; flag +apprenticeship_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  _effects: skill; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +workers_youth_work_trade; mood 0.03_

  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06000000000000005 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; reputation 0.030000000000000002; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +neglected_own_studies_

  _effects: mood 0.12; flag +first_love_reciprocated; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60_

  _effects: cash -2 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); flag +clinic_fee_recorded_as_debt; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; knowledge “the outpatient fee can be recorded against next month’s wage”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Emil把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Hermann Kral站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Emil手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; reputation 0.03_

  猴子走在她前面，红绳在项圈后面拖着一截。乐师在面包店台阶上坐下，从兜里掏出一块干硬的面包掰成两半，一半给了猴子。他说这条巷子里只有面包店老板娘肯让他靠墙歇脚。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; skill_

  Emil把笔推过去，要来人在单子上写下姓名和地址。Katharina Kral收起纸，把帽子压低，从后门走了，那晚机器一次也没开。第二天Hermann Weiss问起，Emil只说人没来。
  _effects: mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  Grete Winter 2在Stein-Wohnung, Josefstadt生下一个孩子，取名Mitzi Bauer。Emil在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Emil从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; cash -6 (midwife fee); role; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:82_

  _effects: cash 4 (first instalment of the family debt); relation kin with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; role; flag +family_debt_settled; knowledge “余款每月在面包房门口交，借条放在饼干盒里。”_

  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Emil攥紧扶手。Hermann Kral站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.06 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.03_

  Emil把传单推回Mitzi手里，说这活不能在我这台机上印。Mitzi收起纸走了，第二天车间里多了一个不说笑的角落。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.01999999999999999_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Emil在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Emil的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `major-task.promotion-request/raise-refused` tags: task, task:work, work, promotion, wage
  Gustav Stein把工票推回来，说厂里今年不加人也不加钱，谁提谁走。Emil还想解释，Gustav Stein已经转身去点货。第二天，车间里有人小声问Emil是不是去账房告过状；Emil的名字被记在工头的本子上。那天下午，Emil被派去地下室搬纸，直到收工。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; flag +marked_as_complainer; role; knowledge “厂里今年不加钱，提加薪的人会被工头记住。”_

  Emil把自己的新鞋留在家里，向Grete Winter 2借了一双旧舞鞋。鞋跟矮半寸，鞋面有一道补过的痕。Grete Winter 2说这双跳过三场婚礼，跟脚。Emil在舞池里转了两圈，果然不疼；Katharina Kral却盯着那双旧鞋看了很久，说鞋跟像上一季的。
  _effects: mood 0.06; condition; flag +wore_borrowed_shoes_to_ball; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; knowledge “the borrowed shoes have danced through three weddings”_

  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; flag +still_looking_for_room_

- **1932.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Emil的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  Emil记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Grete Winter 2说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Grete Winter 2陪着Emil去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。

  厂里把一张短工名单贴在厂门口。Emil的名字在第三行，旁边写着“下周等通知”。等到月底，通知没有来，工票也停了。
  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

  Grete Winter 2把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Emil留在Stein-Wohnung, Josefstadt，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Emil在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods); mood -0.06; role_

  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1933.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Emil在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1934.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-failed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Helene Bauer差三分。回家路上，Helene Bauer一句话也没说，进了门就把自己关在屋里。Emil把饭菜放在门口，敲了两下，没有回应。半夜，Emil听见屋里有人在翻书，翻到天亮。第二天清早，Helene Bauer出来洗脸，眼睛肿着，问Emil能不能再教一年。补考费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001147:child_

  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004297_

  _effects: cash -2 (门厅里的异国军靴); mood -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +quartering_order_received; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; knowledge “the billeting order takes one street-facing room per househo”_

  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.06000000000000005 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

  你没去借钱，直接下楼找房东，说月底前先付两克朗，剩下两克朗分两个星期补齐。房东叼着雪茄，拿铅笔在你的纸条背面写了个日期，说再拖一天就换锁。邻居在楼道口听见了，冲你摇了摇头，但没说什么。

  Emil开口借钱读夜校，Helene Bauer坐在新买的沙发上，说家里也有难处，孩子要上学。Emil看见茶几上放着一盒没拆的进口糖，没有提。临走时，Helene Bauer送到门口，说以后少来往，省得两边都为难。

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  Rosa Lechner把进口试剂的关税单摊开，说没有海关章就不能算研究支出。Emil在抽屉底层找到三张，边角被福尔马林浸黄。Katharina Stein站在门口，第一次没有插话。Rosa Lechner把单据一张张贴进账本，说“这次算你的”。

  包厢里已经坐了五个人，Katharina Berg把一只藤箱放在第六个座位上，说箱子里是易碎品。Luise Hofer查票时数了数人头，让Emil站到走廊去，说“票上没写座位”。Katharina Berg低头看报，藤箱占着座位，一路没有挪。

  _effects: cash 12 (卖掉烟铺的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; reputation -0.05; condition; flag +sold_the_tobacco_shop; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

  _effects: cash -5 (电车轨道上的苹果车); health -0.01 (电车轨道上的苹果车); condition; flag +accident_in_blackout; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子)_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; reputation -0.020000000000000004; health -0.02 (在湿冷街头耗到傍晚)_

  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); health -0.019999999999999997 (牙病拖延未治); condition_

  _effects: cash -4 (请假三天损失的工钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.1 (在家照护后腿肿消退); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03_

  Emil把钱包塞进内袋，走了两条街才敢回头看。三克朗够买一周的煤，彩票也许什么都不是；但那天晚上，Emil把彩票压在盐罐底下，像压着一张还没有兑现的明天。

  _effects: flag +blocked_car_door_for_refugee_family; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the stationmaster recorded the carriage number”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1945.12** `r1-hc.pickpocket::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::pickpocket-crisis-crowded-tram/pickpocket-crisis-empty-purse` tags: street, pickpocket, tram, theft, wages, vienna, urban, crisis
  Emil摸到钱包还在，里面的钱却没了，只剩一张电车月票。月票上被人用指甲划了一道，像是留个记号；Emil把空钱包举给售票员看，售票员说，今天第三起了。
  _effects: cash -8 (电车上的第三只手); flag +robbed_on_a_crowded_tram; role; knowledge “the thief leaves the wallet and takes only the notes”_

- **1946.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-shop-and-the-sisters` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  遗嘱把烟铺、楼上的两位老小姐和十四克朗的赊账一起留给Emil。Alois Lechner第一次见面就叫Emil“侄子”，把钥匙、账本和一只总在柜台下打盹的猫交过来。Emil原本只想要一个家，现在有了一个需要每天清晨六点开门的家；Hermann Bauer在门口抽完一支雪茄，提醒Emil月底先还利息。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (先还一部分烟铺赊账（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; reputation 0.020000000000000004; condition; flag +kept_the_tobacco_shop; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:af17-lucky-idiot/windfall-talk` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  咖啡馆里有人问Emil，是不是替哪个议员跑过腿，或者认识一个会看牌的女人。Emil说自己只是运气好；这句话说出口，连Emil自己都觉得像在遮掩。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 50 -> 62; reputation -0.020000000000000004; flag +luck_explained_as_a_deal; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009103_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/small-mercies` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  又一年过去，Emil在煤气灯下捡到一枚银币，又在当铺门口遇见一个认错人的老主顾。好事都不大，却一件接一件；街角咖啡馆里的人开始说，Emil一定知道什么别人不知道的门路。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 1 -> 2; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 62 -> 68; cash 1 (捡到的银币); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/soup-kitchen-opened` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil把工会活动室的长桌拼起来，用两口大锅煮扁豆汤。Ferdinand Hofer 2赊了半袋豆子和一筐洋葱，条件是罢工结束后先还他。第一天来了三十七个人，Emil给每个碗里多舀半勺；汤不够时，就往锅里再添一壶水。
  _effects: cash -1 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; health 0.019999999999999997 (罢工第七天的汤锅); flag +work13_strike_soup_kitchen; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; organization_funds; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1946.02** `r2-faith.vagrancy-arrest::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/testified-to-residence` tags: charity, vagrancy, arrest, workhouse, testimony, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa Lechner问Maria Kral在哪儿住，Emil说就在后院那间偏房，住了六年，房租每月初一交。Rosa Lechner翻到登记页，发现门牌号少了一个字母，还是签了。Maria Kral出来时，把手里攥着的一小块肥皂塞给Emil，说刚从收容所领的。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +testified_for_neighbor_against_vagrancy_charge; role; relation friend with person-maria-kral; knowledge “the neighbor has rented the back room for six years”_

- **1946.05** `fa17-cabaret.debut::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/held-the-room` tags: art, performance, cabaret, debut, reputation, failure, theatre, vienna
  首演夜，Emil在第二首歌的中间忘了两句词，干脆停下来听乐队多走了一个小节，再接着唱。台下有人以为这是新加的停顿，笑了一声，随后整间屋子都放松了。Hermann Bauer在后台说，今晚之后，节目单上可以印Emil的名字。
  _effects: state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:224; cash 2 (首演酬劳与加演分成); role; reputation 0.06; skill; flag +debut_success; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127_

- **1946.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Emil Moser把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Emil去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1947.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/keeps-home` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil没有给Maria Bauer报名。家里需要一双能看摊的手，学校也还欠着上一学期的纸笔钱；晚上Maria Bauer把别人的课本借来看，Emil假装没有看见。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 16 -> 8; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 40 -> 40; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 6 -> 6; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> false; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash 1 (the child's small earnings at the stall); flag +child_kept_from_school_

- **1947.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Emil Moser 2在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-62; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:af17-lucky-idiot/luck-year/the-bill-comes-due` tags: af17-lucky-idiot, luck, fortune, windfall, suspicion, social, coincidence, absurd
  好运气没有出现。Emil错过电车，鞋跟断在环城大道的石缝里，回家才发现钥匙也丢了。邻居没有安慰，只说“总算轮到你了”，语气像在结一笔旧账。
  _effects: state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.streak 2 -> 1; state_parameter af17-lucky-idiot.suspicion 68 -> 78; cash -1 (修鞋和配钥匙)_

- **1947.01** `kernel-health.task-house-call::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::house-call-medical-students/house-call-medical-ambulance` tags: health, task, house_call, care, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Emil被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission); health 0.05 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”_

- **1947.02** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  负责人说这是不信任，Emil回说数目对得上才叫信任。Katharina Kral 3把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; reputation 0.04; skill_

- **1947.03** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009588_

- **1947.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  来问入会的是个瘦高的年轻人，帽檐捏在手里，说自己在第十六区的织布厂做了半年。Emil把名册翻到空白页，蘸水笔尖在“住址”那栏停了一下，等他报出街名。老成员在角落里把账本合上，声音很轻。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; skill_

- **1948.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Emil的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Emil看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009325_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Emil一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Berta Kral送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1948.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/sold-the-shop` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  房产经纪人只用了两天就找到买主，要把烟铺改成一家卖进口香皂的店面。Emil在契约上签了字，拿到十二克朗，比继承来的赊账多出不少。Emil Moser和她的姐姐在月底搬去城郊的侄女家，临走前把猫留在空柜台下面；Emil没有回去看那只猫，只在账本最后一页写下“已结清”。
  _effects: cash 12 (卖掉烟铺的钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000201:child; reputation -0.05_

- **1948.01** `r3-crime.poisoning-case::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital::one-microscope/drank-from-the-pot` tags: crime, medical, poison, marriage, suspicion, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil先盛了一碗，说“我先尝咸淡”。半个钟头后，Emil开始呕吐，Elisabeth Lechner跑去叫Elisabeth Berg。洗胃时，Emil听见Mitzi在门外问“谁做的汤”，Elisabeth Lechner说“是他自己”。Elisabeth Berg把铜勺放进证物袋，说“先查查锅”。
  _effects: cash -6 (汤锅边搁着一把铜勺); condition; flag +poisoned_by_own_soup; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001426:child; knowledge “the soup was poisoned before the subject tasted it”_

  Mitzi Winter端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Emil家的炉子捅旺。Emil靠在床头，听Mitzi Winter讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health 0.04 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000969:child_

  _effects: flag +factory_rejected_

  _effects: cash -4 (药柜上的两瓶糖浆); health 0.05 (药柜上的两瓶糖浆); flag +bought_full_medicine_course; knowledge “the cough syrup must be taken three times a day”_

- **1949.06** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Luise Hofer 2在午休时把本子摊在Emil的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Emil从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Luise Hofer 2当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-127; reputation 0.04_

- **1949.07** `r14-fam.iron-brace::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/kept-behind-the-curtain` tags: family, children, disability, disabled_child_care, care, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil没有去领支架，只把Anton Bauer留在屋里，说外面的人会盯着看。白天窗帘拉着一半，Anton Bauer趴在窗台上看院子里的孩子踢球，膝盖顶着玻璃。Emil下班回来，发现窗台上有一小片被呼吸蒙白的雾，擦了又会出现。
  _effects: cash -2 (夜里的铁支架); condition; flag +hid_disabled_child_

  Emil只去了那一次。房间是借的，床单洗得发白；两个人事后都没有提下一次。Emil回到街上时，天还没黑，卖报的孩子正在喊一个陌生的名字。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003199:child; cash -1 (the borrowed room and the coffee); mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +one_borrowed_night_

  钳子夹住牙根时，Emil听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Mitzi Winter扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Elisabeth Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Luise Bauer。Emil在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Emil从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。

  台阶上蹲着几个等消息的人，Emil Hofer把烟盒纸摊在膝盖上画路线，说北站货栈的管事认字就行，不认登记所那一套。Emil把那半截铅笔头借来，在纸角写上时辰，折好收进内袋。

  Emil Hofer把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Emil在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Elisabeth Moser 2分给Emil半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +work13_turned_away_at_factory_gate; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”_

- **1951.06** `r14-work.embezzlement::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-to-sign-the-false-entry` tags: work, embezzlement, crime, debt, money, shame, vienna, urban
  Emil Stein让Emil在一张没有凭证的支出单上签字，说年底一起补。Emil把笔放下，说这笔账对不上。Emil Stein看了Emil一会儿，把单子收回抽屉，说那就换个人签。Emil知道自己在账房的位子坐不久了。
  _effects: cash -1 (账本上的一笔空白（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.020000000000000018; reputation 0.04; flag +refused_false_entry; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “the owner keeps unsigned expense slips in his drawer”_

- **1951.09** `r2-law.lottery-ticket::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ticket-lost` tags: finance, lottery, gambling, luck, neighbors, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil把彩票夹在烟盒里，烟盒又随手给了Grete Winter 2。开奖那天，Emil翻遍所有口袋，只找到一张空烟盒。Grete Winter 2说烟盒已经扔进炉子，Emil看着炉膛里的灰，想起号码好像开头是七；Emil Stein说没有票，号码对上了也不能兑。
  _effects: cash -2 (中奖号码写在烟盒上（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +lost_lottery_ticket_in_stove; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; knowledge “a winning number is worthless without the ticket”_

- **1951.10** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Ferdinand Berg手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Ferdinand Berg把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Emil记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food_

- **1952.01** `fm17-seance.parlor-table::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-rapping-stops` tags: faith, ritual, seance, grief, spiritualism, money, doubt, vienna
  桌面响了三下，又响了三下。Otto Lechner说是死者在敲门，可问到最后，没有一个名字、日期或物件对得上。Otto Lechner擦着额头说，有时候灵只说一半；Emil看着窗帘缝里漏进来的街灯，忽然觉得那三下更像有人在楼下关车门。
  _effects: cash -2 (降神会的费用（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_add fm17-miracle-witness state:world-vienna-1912:291; flag +fm17_seance_unexplained; flag +fm17_miracle_witnessed; knowledge “我看见的那件事，到今天我仍说不出它是什么。”_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Emil在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 1 -> 2_

- **1952.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.give-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Emil只喝了半杯就看见门口站着等位的老主顾，手里转着帽子。Emil把报纸叠好放回木架，起身让出窗边的位子，一杯 Melange 三十赫勒，侍者点头致意，没再收第二杯的钱。走出门时雨小了些，Emil站在檐下理了理衣领，觉得这半个下午也不算白来。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (一杯 Melange 的价钱); reputation 0.03; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.02** `r3-crime.victim-family::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/case-dropped` tags: crime, family, grief, court, settlement, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi Huber 2第二次来，没有进门，只站在台阶上说证据不够。Klara问“那天的血衣呢”，Mitzi Huber 2说“在证物室，但编号对不上”。灵堂里的蜡烛烧到了底，Josef Moser把状纸收起来，折成四折，塞进供桌下面。
  _effects: condition; flag +case_dropped_for_lack_of_evidence; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142; knowledge “the bloodstained coat lost its evidence number”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142_

- **1952.05** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil数出八赫勒交给Klara，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Klara说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Emil盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); flag +funeral_society_member_

- **1952.08** `vienna-church-community.saint-vincent-box/box-request-granted` tags: faith, social, family, home
  圣文生会的修女在登记簿上写下Emil的门牌号，递过面包券和一小袋土豆。Katharina Winter在旁边按了指印作保，说这户男人在工厂有活。
  _effects: food; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-shop-and-the-sisters` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  遗嘱把烟铺、楼上的两位老小姐和十四克朗的赊账一起留给Emil。Rosa Lechner第一次见面就叫Emil“侄子”，把钥匙、账本和一只总在柜台下打盹的猫交过来。Emil原本只想要一个家，现在有了一个需要每天清晨六点开门的家；Katharina Berg在门口抽完一支雪茄，提醒Emil月底先还利息。
  _effects: cash -2 (先还一部分烟铺赊账（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; reputation 0.02; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `r3-crime.crime-scene::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/second-trail-to-the-cellar` tags: crime, police, evidence, street, mistake, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil在院墙下面发现另一串鞋印，比死者的鞋小两号，一直通到Alois Weiss 2家的煤窖。Ferdinand 2让人把窖门打开，里面只有一排腌菜缸。Alois Weiss 2站在门口说“你们随便看”，手却一直攥着围裙角。
  _effects: condition; flag +found_a_second_trail; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108; knowledge “a second set of footprints leads to the neighbor’s coal cell”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-108_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/carried` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Emil又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 2 -> 3; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_expire:fm17-miracle-witness` tags: fm17-miracle-witness, faith, miracle, vision, witness, religion, reputation, doubt
  Emil的「见过无法解释之事的人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove fm17-miracle-witness state:world-vienna-1912:291_

- **1953.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.borrow-handcart/handcart-and-ten` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  星期天搬家的前一晚，有人坐在印厂友人家的厨房里把事说全：车要借，十克朗也要借。对方合上账本，从抽屉里数出两张五克朗，说月底先还三克朗，剩下的按月摊。手推车的轮轴有点响，搬之前得先上油。
  _effects: cash 10 (友人借给搬家应急的十克朗); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003199:child; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149_

- **1953.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Katharina Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Katharina Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Katharina Kral 3攥着Emil的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1954.01** `vienna-family-fatherhood/child-born-at-home` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Elisabeth Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个孩子，取名Anna Bauer。Emil在厨房和走廊之间来回，接生的人让烧水、拿布、付六克朗；孩子天亮后睡着了，Emil从此在户籍表上多了“父亲”一栏，手背上还留着烫水壶的红印。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.05 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:315_

  Emil的「丧亲之痛」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1954.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.01000000000000012_

- **1954.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kin-nursed` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, postwar
  Elisabeth Lechner把粥熬得很稀，一勺一勺吹凉，托着Emil的后颈喂下去。窗帘拉开半尺，太阳从床脚移到墙上，Emil第一次觉得退烧后的骨头是轻的。Elisabeth Lechner把药钱记在日历背面，说下个月少买两次肉。
  _effects: cash -2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); food; health 0.08 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_kin_care_

- **1954.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.ask-master` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Emil把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.039999999999999994; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; skill_

- **1954.08** `r1-civ.harbouring-fugitive::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/turned-away-but-fed` tags: politics, hiding, fugitive, neighbor, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil没有开门，只隔着门板说家里有孩子发烧。Katharina Kral 3在门外站了一会儿，把一张写着地址的纸条从门缝塞进来。第二天，Emil照着地址送了一包面包，屋子里已经空了，桌上留着一只没洗的杯子。
  _effects: cash -1 (半夜的第三下敲门（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.12; flag +turned_away_fugitive_but_sent_food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; knowledge “the fugitive left an address under the door”_

- **1955.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Emil的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Emil看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012272_

- **1955.01** `r13-edu.correspondence-course::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/certificate-by-post` tags: education, correspondence, adult_education, literacy, mail, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil每周收到一叠油印讲义，做完题再寄回去。Gustav Hofer在页边用红笔改错，有时附一句“第二步再想想”。十二个月后，Emil收到结业证书，纸很薄，但编号印得清楚。证书被Emil钉在床头，邮差再来时，问Emil要不要报下一门课。编号是手写的。
  _effects: flag +correspondence_certificate; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1955.09** `r14-work.criminal-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-the-back-door` tags: crime, criminal_offer, risk, money, temptation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand 2把条件说完，Emil把手插回口袋，说这门生意找别人。Ferdinand 2没有生气，只把烟头踩灭，说这条街上不缺愿意干的人。Emil走出巷子，才发现后背的衬衫已经湿透。Helene Kral 2在街角等着，问谈得怎么样。
  _effects: cash -1 (仓库后门的提议（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +refused_criminal_offer; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000912:child; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; knowledge “the fixer keeps a list of men who owe him money”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1955.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Friedrich Stein说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Friedrich Stein陪着Emil去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141_

- **1955.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Emil把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Elisabeth Lechner把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001426:child; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

- **1955.11** `vienna-market-household.market-credit/market-credit.pay-later` tags: home, market, food, social
  Emil捏着三个克朗硬币站在玛丽亚的菜摊前，土豆和洋葱已经装进布袋。周五结清时，玛丽亚用粉笔在小黑板上划掉名字，Friedrich Stein点点头。
  _effects: cash -3 (偿还市场赊账（其中3克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-141_

- **1956.01** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Hermann Weiss 2凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Emil把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-161; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-161_

- **1956.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/cupboard-again` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Emil刚提一句“那年分东西”，Helene Bauer就把勺子放下，说柜子是他先看中的，凭什么让。话越说越硬，汤还剩半碗，Helene Bauer起身拿了帽子，椅子在石地上刮出一声。Emil一个人坐到教堂的钟敲完，把两份饭钱都付了。
  _effects: relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001147:child; cash -0.4 (付了两份饭钱（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Emil对估价不服，找Mitzi Stein 3写申诉，说母牛去年就死了。Katharina Huber要求死牛证明；兽医的证明在村里，村长说档案柜的钥匙在Katharina Huber手里。Emil跑了两趟，最后把申诉和一张村长签字的纸条一起递上去；Katharina Huber收下，说等三个月。
  _effects: cash -8 (遗产清单上的犁（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +appealed_inheritance_tax_assessment; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174; knowledge “the village records are locked in the auditor’s own cabinet”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-174; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146_

- **1956.02** `r2-law.citizenship-papers::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/oath-administered` tags: law, citizenship, papers, exam, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Ferdinand 2问Emil国歌第二句是什么，又让他说出本区议员的名字。Emil答对了一半，Ferdinand 2在表格上写“基本合格”，让下周来宣誓。Ferdinand Berg在门外等着，手里拿着一面小旗；Emil出来时，Ferdinand 2又在后面补了一句：别迟到。
  _effects: cash -8 (入籍宣誓前的口试（其中8克朗记入债务）); flag +citizenship_oath_scheduled; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89; knowledge “the oath ceremony is held every second Tuesday”_

- **1956.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Elisabeth Hofer把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Emil去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1957.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Gustav Stein 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Gustav Stein 2在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-164; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-164_

- **1957.01** `r15-fam.child-leaves::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/stays-for-now` tags: family, child, leaving, home, child_left_home, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Bauer说再等一年，等家里的债还掉一点。Emil没有劝，只把晚饭多添了一勺；夜里Emil听见Maria Bauer在隔壁翻书，煤油灯一直亮到后半夜。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 24 -> 29; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 52 -> 56; cash -1 (another year of food and lamp oil（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.01** `r13-fam.keep-family-secret::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/secret-exposed` tags: task, family, secret, reputation, sibling, fear, vienna, urban
  Grete Kral不知道从哪里听说了纸上的名字，整条走廊都在传。Emil回家时，Klara正把柜子里的东西翻了一地，说早该烧掉。两人吵到半夜，Emil把纸摊在灯下，第一次读完最后一行；纸上的日期比Emil记得的早了三年。
  _effects: reputation -0.12; condition; flag +family_secret_exposed; relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the paper is three years older than the family claimed”_

- **1957.05** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013712_

  登记处的老职员把Elisabeth Bauer的名字写成了Emil小时候的名字。Emil指出错误，对方却说“你们家的人长得都一样”；Elisabeth Bauer拿着写错名字的课程表回家，一路上都在偷笑。

  Emil家分期买了一台电视，先付三十五先令，余款分十二个月。Anton Lechner当晚就带着孩子来看节目，客厅里坐满了人，窗台上晾的袜子忘了收。
  _effects: cash -35 (first television instalment（其中35克朗记入债务）); mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +television_at_home_
